How long does the $11{:}44$ from Birmingham take to get to York?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken}           &= 14{:}15 - 11{:}44 \\\\
                            &= 2 \ \text{hours} \ 31 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                            &= 2 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 31  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                            &= 120 + 31  \\\\
                            &= 151  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$151  \ \text{minutes}$

<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

How many minutes did the crossing from Dover to Calais actually 
take?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned} 
\text{Time taken} &=  09{:}11 - 08{:}02 \\\\
&= 1 \ \text{hour} \ 9 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= 1 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} \ 9 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= 60 + 9 \\\\
&= 69 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$69 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned} 
\text{Time taken} &=  09{:}11 - 08{:}02 \\\\
&= 1 \ \text{hour} \ 9 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= 1 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} \ 9 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= 60 + 9 \\\\
&= 69 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>

<div class='answercodeblock'>

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

How many minutes late did the ferry arrive in Calais?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time delayed }  &= 09{:}11 - 07{:}45 - 1 \dfrac {1} {4} \ \text{hour} \\\\
                      &= 1 \ \text{hour} \  26 \ \text{minutes} - 1 \ \text{hour} \ 15 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                      &=  11 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$11 \ \text{ minutes}$

</div>
</div>

                      &= 1 \ \text{hour} \  26 \ \text{minutes} - 1 \ \text{hour} \ 15 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                      &=  11 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>

<div class='question subquestion'>

She starts the swimming stage at $12{:}45 \ \text{pm}$. If she takes $79 \ \text{minutes}$ to complete this stage, at what time does she start the cycling stage.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Cycling start time}   &= 12{:}45 \ \text{pm} + 79 \ \text{minutes}  \\\\
                            &= 12{:}45 \ \text{pm} + 1 \ \text{hour } 19 \ \text{minutes}  \\\\
                            &=  2{:}04 \ \text{pm}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$2{:}04 \ \text{pm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}

                            &= 12{:}45 \ \text{pm} + 1 \ \text{hour } 19 \ \text{minutes}  \\\\
                            &=  2{:}04 \ \text{pm}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

If she starts the running stage at $4{:}38 \ \text{pm}$ work out how long she took in minutes on the cycling stage.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken}   &= 4{:}38 \ \text{pm} - 2{:}04 \ \text{pm} \\\\
                    &= 2 \ \text{hours} \ 34 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                    &= 2 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 34 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                    &= 120 + 34 \\\\
                    &= 154 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$154 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken}   &= 4{:}38 \ \text{pm} - 2{:}04 \ \text{pm} \\\\
                    &= 2 \ \text{hours} \ 34 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                    &= 2 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 34 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                    &= 120 + 34 \\\\
                    &= 154 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$154 \ \text{minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

If she took $107 \ \text{minutes}$ on the running stage work out how long she took to complete the whole triathlon. Give your answer in hours and minutes.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total time taken} &=  79 \ \text{minutes} + 154 \ \text{minutes} + 107 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                        &=  340 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {340} {60} \ \text{hours} \\\\
                        &= 5 \dfrac {40} {60} \ \text{hours} \\\\
                        &= 5 \ \text{hours } 40 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$5 \  \text{hours} \ 40 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total time taken} &=  79 \ \text{minutes} + 154 \ \text{minutes} + 107 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                        &=  340 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {340} {60} \ \text{hours} \\\\
                        &= 5 \dfrac {40} {60} \ \text{hours} \\\\
                        &= 5 \ \text{hours } 40 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>

<div class='question question'>

It gains $4 \ \text{minutes}$ every $3 \ \text{hours}$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>

<div class='working placeholder'>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What time does the watch show when the correct time is $12$ noon on Friday, assuming the watch has not been reset again?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time Gained}     &= 48 \times \dfrac {4} {3} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {16 \times 3 \times 4} {3} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {16 \times \cancel 3 \times 4} {\cancel 3} \\\\
                       &= 16 \times 4 \\\\
                       &= 64 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time shown}      &= \text{Actual time} + \text{Time gained} \\\\
                       &= 12{:}00 \ \text{pm} +64 \ \text{minutes}\\\\
                       &= 01{:}04 \ \text{pm}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$01{:}04 \ \text{pm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time Gained}     &= 48 \times \dfrac {4} {3} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {16 \times 3 \times 4} {3} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {16 \times \cancel 3 \times 4} {\cancel 3} \\\\
                       &= 16 \times 4 \\\\
                       &= 64 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time shown}      &= \text{Actual time} + \text{Time gained} \\\\
                       &= 12{:}00 \ \text{pm} +64 \ \text{minutes}\\\\
                       &= 01{:}04 \ \text{pm}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$01{:}04 \ \text{pm}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

The watch is reset again to show the correct time at $12$ noon on Friday. 
What is the correct time when the watch is showing $19{:}40$ on Friday night?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the time passed since 12 noon = a


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time shown}    &= \text{Correct time} + \text{Time gained} \\\\
19{:}40  &= (12{:}00 + a) + (a) \times \dfrac {4} {3} \\\\
19{:}40  &=  12{:}00 +  a \times \dfrac{7} {3} \\\\
a        &= ( 19{:}40 - 12{:}00 ) \times \dfrac {3} {7} \\\\
a        &= (07 {:} 40) \times \dfrac {3} {7} \\\\
a        &= 03{:}17
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Correct time}    &=  12{:}00 \ \text{pm} + 03{:}17 \\\\
                       &=  15{:}07
\end{aligned}
$


</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$15{:}07 $

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the time passed since 12 noon = a

a        &= ( 19{:}40 - 12{:}00 ) \times \dfrac {3} {7} \\\\
a        &= (07 {:} 40) \times \dfrac {3} {7} \\\\
a        &= 03{:}17
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Correct time}    &=  12{:}00 \ \text{pm} + 03{:}17 \\\\
                       &=  15{:}07
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<!-- - if !options[:questions_only] -->
<!-- = render :reviewsubtag, question: question -->
<div class='question question'>

Abigail gets up at $07{:}40$ in the morning. 

$25 \ \text{minutes}$ later she starts her breakfast, which takes $10 \ \text{minutes}$ to finish. 

She then leaves for school.  Her journey takes $35 \ \text{minutes}$ in total. 

She arrives $5 \ \text{minutes}$ late for registration. 

At what time is registration?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Registration time}      &= 7{:}40 \ \text{am} + 25 \ \text{minutes} + 10 \ \text{minutes} + 35 \ \text{minutes} - 5 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                              &= 7{:}40 \ \text{am} + 70 \ \text{minutes} - 5 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                              &= 7{:}40 \ \text{am} + 65 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                              &= 8{:}45 \ \text{am}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$8{:}45 \ \text{am}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Registration time}      &= 7{:}40 \ \text{am} + 25 \ \text{minutes} + 10 \ \text{minutes} + 35 \ \text{minutes} - 5 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                              &= 7{:}40 \ \text{am} + 70 \ \text{minutes} - 5 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                              &= 7{:}40 \ \text{am} + 65 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                              &= 8{:}45 \ \text{am}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$8{:}45 \ \text{am}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<!-- - if !options[:questions_only] -->
<!-- = render :reviewsubtag, question: question -->
<div class='question question'>

A clock is $18 \ \text{minutes}$ slow, but is gaining $7 \dfrac{1}{2} \ \text{seconds}$ every $\text{hour}$. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

How long will it take for the clock to show the correct time?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken to show correct time}  &= \dfrac{Time lag}{Time gain every hour} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac{18 \times 60} {7 \dfrac{1}{2}} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac{18 \times 60}{\dfrac{15}{2}} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac{18 \times 60 \times 2}{15} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac{18 \times 15 \times 4 \times 2}{15} \\\\
                                        &= 18 \times 4 \times 2 \\\\
\text{Time taken to show correct time}  &= 144 \ \text{hours}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$144 \ \text{hours}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken to show correct time}  &= \dfrac{Time lag}{Time gain every hour} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac{18 \times 60} {7 \dfrac{1}{2}} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac{18 \times 60}{\dfrac{15}{2}} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac{18 \times 60 \times 2}{15} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac{18 \times 15 \times 4 \times 2}{15} \\\\
                                        &= 18 \times 4 \times 2 \\\\
\text{Time taken to show correct time}  &= 144 \ \text{hours}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$144 \ \text{hours}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Another clock is $30 \ \text{minutes}$ fast, but is losing $11 \dfrac{1}{4} \ \text{seconds per hour}$.

How many minutes fast will the second clock be when the first clock shows the right time?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken to show correct time}  &= \dfrac{Time fast}{Time loss every hour} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac{30 \times 60} {11 \dfrac{1}{4}} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac{30 \times 60}{\dfrac{45}{4}} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac{30 \times 60 \times 4}{45} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac{3 \times 10 \times 15 \times 4 \times 4}{3 \times 15} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac{\cancel{3} \times 10 \times \cancel{15} \times 4 \times 4}{\cancel{3} \times \cancel{15}} \\\\
                                        &= 10 \times 4 \times 4 \\\\
\text{Time taken to show correct time}  &= 160 \ \text{hours}
\end{aligned}
$

Time difference between first and second clock = $160 - 144 \ \text{hours}$
                                               = $16 \ \text{hours}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$16 \ \text{hours}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken to show correct time}  &= \dfrac{Time fast}{Time loss every hour} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac{30 \times 60} {11 \dfrac{1}{4}} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac{30 \times 60}{\dfrac{45}{4}} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac{30 \times 60 \times 4}{45} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac{3 \times 10 \times 15 \times 4 \times 4}{3 \times 15} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac{\cancel{3} \times 10 \times \cancel{15} \times 4 \times 4}{\cancel{3} \times \cancel{15}} \\\\
                                        &= 10 \times 4 \times 4 \\\\
\text{Time taken to show correct time}  &= 160 \ \text{hours}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Time difference between first and second clock = $160 - 144 \ \text{hours}$
                                               = $16 \ \text{hours}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$16 \ \text{hours}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
</ul>

</div>

                                &= 18{:}00- 04{:}25 \\\\
                                &= 13{:}35
\end{aligned}
$

<!-- - if !options[:questions_only] -->

<div class='question question'>

How many seconds are there in a day?

- $3600$
- $1400$
- $1440$
- $51{,}840$
- $86{,}400$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1 \ \text{day} \\\\
&= 24 \ \text{hours} \\\\
&= 24 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= 24 \times 60 \times 60 \ \text{seconds} \\\\
&= 24 \times 3600 \\\\
&= 86400  \ \text{seconds}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='option'>
<p>E</p>
</div>
<div class='answer'>

$86400  \ \text{seconds}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1 \ \text{day} \\\\
&= 24 \ \text{hours} \\\\
&= 24 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= 24 \times 60 \times 60 \ \text{seconds} \\\\
&= 24 \times 3600 \\\\
&= 86400  \ \text{seconds}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>E</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$86400  \ \text{seconds}$
</code></pre>
</div>

Tom goes to a theme park.

There are $4$ activities at the theme park.

The table gives information about these activities.

|   **Activity**   |         **Start times**        | **Time taken by activity (minutes)** |
|:----------------:|:------------------------------:|:------------------------------------:|
|  Penguin feeding | $10{:}45 \ 12{:}15 \ 13{:}45$  |                 $35$                  |
|    Jeep safari   | $10{:}45 \ 12{:}15 \ 13{:}45$  |                  $45$                  |
| Steam train ride | $10{:}45 \ 12{:}15 \ 13{:}45$  |                  $25$                  |
|   Dolphin show   | $10{:}45 \ 12{:}15 \ 13{:}45$  |                  $40$                  |

Tom wants to do all $4$ activities.

He arrives at the theme park at $10{:}20$. 

He needs to leave the theme park by $14{:}30$.

Plan a schedule for Tom's visit to the theme park so he can do all $4$ activities.

| **Activity** | **Start times** | **Time taken by activity (minutes)** |
|:------------:|:---------------:|:------------------------------------:|
|              |                 |                                      |
|              |                 |                                      |
|              |                 |                                      |
|              |                 |                                      |

&   \text{Jeep safari}     &   12{:}00   &   12{:}45 \\
&   \text{Steam train ride}     &   14{:}00   &   14{:}25 \\
&   \text{Dolphin show}     &   13{:}00   &   13{:}40 
\end{array}
$

&   \text{Steam train ride}     &   14{:}00   &   14{:}25 \\

<div class='question question'>

How many days are there in $48 \ \text{weeks}$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>


$
\begin{aligned}
&= 48 \ \text{weeks} \\\\
&= 48 \times 7 \ \text{days} \\\\
&= 336 \ \text{days}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$336 \ \text{days}$

</div>
</div>

<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>
$
\begin{aligned}

\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

John decides to phone his Great Aunt Matilda at $2300 \ \text{hours}$ UK time on the $30 ^ \text{th}$ January. 
What is the time and date in Sydney when Matilda receives the call?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Time in Australia}   &= 23{:}00 + 11 \ \text {hours} \\\\
                            &= 10{:}00
\end{aligned}
$

Matilda receives the call in Australia at $10{:}00$ on $31 \text{st}$ January.


</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$10{:}00$

</div>
<div class='answer'>

$31 \text{st January}$ 

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}

                            &= 10{:}00
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Matilda receives the call in Australia at $10{:}00$ on $31 \text{st}$ January.
<emptyline>
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$31 \text{st January}$ 
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

John's sister Penny goes one better and decides to visit Great Aunt Matilda. 
She leaves the UK at the same time that John makes his phone call. Given that the total journey time is $27 \ \text{hours}$,
 what is the Sydney time and date when Penny arrives?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Arrival time}       &= 23{:}00 + 27 \ \text {hours} + 11 \ \text {hours} \\\\
                          &= 23{:}00 + 38 \ \text {hours} \\\\
                          & = 13{:}00
\end{aligned}
$

Penny arrives in Sydney at $13{:}00$ and on $1 \text {st February}$.

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$13{:}00$

</div>
<div class='answer'>

$1 \text{st February}$ 

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Arrival time}       &= 23{:}00 + 27 \ \text {hours} + 11 \ \text {hours} \\\\
                          &= 23{:}00 + 38 \ \text {hours} \\\\
                          & = 13{:}00
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Penny arrives in Sydney at $13{:}00$ and on $1 \text {st February}$.
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$13{:}00$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$1 \text{st February}$ 
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>

|   Farringdon   | $0722$ | $0732$ | $0752$ | $0800$ | $0804$ | $0816$ | $0824$ |

<div class='workings'>

What is the time of the latest train that someone could catch from St Albans in order to arrive at

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

The train which reaches the City before $08{:}00$ is the train reaching at $07{:}55$.

The $07{:}55$ train start at St Albans at $07{:}28$.

To reach the City no later than eight o'clock, someone should catch from St Albans the train at $07{:}28$. 

</div>

<div class='answer'>

Roweena lives in St Albans and needs to arrive at St Pancras in order to catch the $0855$ train to Paris. 
There is a $30 \ \text{minute}$ check-in at St Pancras International station and she allows herself an extra
 $10 \ \text{minutes}$ 
to change platforms. 
Roweena knows that it takes her $25 \ \text{minutes}$ to walk from her house to get to the platform at St Albans station. 
What is the latest time that she needs to leave her house?

</div>
<div class='workings'>

                                                             & = 08{:}15
\end{aligned}
$

The train reaching St Pancras  before $0815$ is the $08{:}12$ train, starting at St Albans at $07{:}44$. 

$
\begin{aligned} 
\text{Latest time she needs to leave}              & = 07{:}44 - 25 \ \text{min} \\\\
                                                   & = 07{:}19
\end{aligned}
$

The latest time that Roweena need to leave her house is $= 07{:}19$

</div>

<!-- = render :reviewsubtag, question: question -->
<div class='question question'>

If March $23 ^ \text{rd}$ is a Monday, on what day of the week is April $2 ^ \text{nd}$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>


$
\begin{aligned}
&= \text {March} \ 23\text{rd} \ \text {Monday} + 7 \ \text {days} + 3 \ \text{days} \\\\
&= \text {March} \ 30\text{th} \ \text {Monday} + 3 \ \text {days} \\\\
&= \text {April} \ 2\text{nd} \ \text {Thursday}
\end{aligned}
$



</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\text {Thursday}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= \text {March} \ 23\text{rd} \ \text {Monday} + 7 \ \text {days} + 3 \ \text{days} \\\\
&= \text {March} \ 30\text{th} \ \text {Monday} + 3 \ \text {days} \\\\
&= \text {April} \ 2\text{nd} \ \text {Thursday}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\text {Thursday}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

A train leaves Stevenage at $0942$ and arrives in York at $1134$. How many **minutes** does the journey take?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken}           &= 11 {:} 34 - 09 {:} 42 \\\\
                            &= 1 \ \text {hour} \ 52 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                            &= 1 \times 60 \ \text {minutes} + \ 52 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                            &= 60 + 52 \\\\
                            &= 112  \ \text {minutes}
\end{aligned}
$


</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$112 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken}           &= 11 {:} 34 - 09 {:} 42 \\\\
                            &= 1 \ \text {hour} \ 52 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                            &= 1 \times 60 \ \text {minutes} + \ 52 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                            &= 60 + 52 \\\\
                            &= 112  \ \text {minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$112 \ \text{minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>

Write this time as a $12\text{-hour}$ clock, using am or pm.

</div>

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$9{:}30 \ \text{pm}$
</code></pre>

<!-- - if !options[:questions_only] -->
<!-- = render :reviewsubtag, question: question -->
<div class='question question'>

The opening hours of my local supermarket are $7 \ \text{am}$ to $9 \ \text{pm}$ Mondays to Saturdays and 
$10{:}30 \ \text{am}$ to $4{:}30 \ \text{pm}$ on Sundays. For how many hours does this supermarket open each week?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total hours}    &= 6 \times ( 9 \ \text{pm} - 7 \ \text{am} ) + 04{:}30 \ \text{pm} - 10{:}30 \ \text{am} \\\\
                      &= 6 \times 14 \ \text{hours} +  6 \ \text{hours} \\\\
                      &= 84 + 6 \\\\
                      &= 90 \ \text{hours}
\end{aligned}
$


</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$90 \ \text {hours}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total hours}    &= 6 \times ( 9 \ \text{pm} - 7 \ \text{am} ) + 04{:}30 \ \text{pm} - 10{:}30 \ \text{am} \\\\
                      &= 6 \times 14 \ \text{hours} +  6 \ \text{hours} \\\\
                      &= 84 + 6 \\\\
                      &= 90 \ \text{hours}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$90 \ \text {hours}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

For breakfast I eat a slice of buttered toast and a cup of coffee. The time taken to complete these activities is as follows:

| Brown toast in toaster | $3 \ \text{minutes}$  |
|:----------------------:|:-------------------:|
|    Butter the toast    |  $1 \ \text{minute}$ |
| Make coffee in machine | $2 \ \text{minutes}$  |


</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>


</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>


</div>
</div>

</div>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Jonathan thinks that the shortest time taken for me to prepare my breakfast is $6 \ \text{minutes}$. Explain briefly why Jonathan is wrong.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Jonathan is wrong because I can manage making a coffee in a machine and toasting the breast in a toaster as a parallel task. 

The coffee making takes $2 \ \text{minutes}$, which I can do while my bread is getting toasted in the toaster, which takes $3 \ \text{minutes}$. 

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\underline {{\text {Jonathan is wrong because I can manage making a coffee in a machine and toasting the breast in a toaster as a parallel task.}}}$ 

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Jonathan is wrong because I can manage making a coffee in a machine and toasting the breast in a toaster as a parallel task. 
<emptyline>
The coffee making takes $2 \ \text{minutes}$, which I can do while my bread is getting toasted in the toaster, which takes $3 \ \text{minutes}$. 
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\underline {{\text {Jonathan is wrong because I can manage making a coffee in a machine and toasting the breast in a toaster as a parallel task.}}}$ 
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is the correct shortest time?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

The shortest time taken by me to prepare my breakfast is,

$
\begin{aligned}
      & = \text {Brown the toast in toaster + Butter the toast} \\\\
      & = 3 + 1                                                 \\\\
      & = 4 \ \text{minutes}                               
\end{aligned}
$

The shortest time taken by me to prepare my breakfast is $=4 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$4 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>

<div class='workingcodeblock'>

\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
The shortest time taken by me to prepare my breakfast is $=4 \ \text{minutes}$
</code></pre>

<!-- - if !options[:questions_only] -->
<!-- = render :reviewsubtag, question: question -->
<div class='question question'>

A concert performance of Mahler's third symphony begins at $7{:}35 \ \text{pm}$ and ends at 
$9{:}11 \ \text{pm}$. 
How many minutes does it last?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Duration}            &= 9{:}11 \ \text{pm} - 7{:}35 \ \text{pm} \\\\
                           &= 1 \ \text{hour} \ 36 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                           &= 1 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 36 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                           &=60 + 36 \\\\
                           &= 96 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$96 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Duration}            &= 9{:}11 \ \text{pm} - 7{:}35 \ \text{pm} \\\\
                           &= 1 \ \text{hour} \ 36 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                           &= 1 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 36 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                           &=60 + 36 \\\\
                           &= 96 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$96 \ \text{minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

The Swiss transportation system is the best in the world. Not only does it run on time but it is possible to transfer seamlessly from one mode of transport to another.

The journey from Giessbach to Grutschalp has three stages: a steamer across Lake Brienzersee to Interlaken, a train from Interlaken to Lauterbrunnen and finally a cable car from Lauterbrunnen to Grutschalp.

-  Steamer boats leave Giessbach every $70 \ \text{minutes}$ and the journey across the lake takes $60 \ \text{minutes}$.
-  Trains leave Interlaken every $40 \ \text{minutes}$ and the journey takes $30 \ \text{minutes}$.  
-  Cable cars leave Lauterbrunnen every $15 \ \text{minutes}$ and the journey takes $10 \ \text{minutes}$.

The first steamer, train and cable car of the day are all at $07{:}00$.

</div>

<div class='question question'>

At a busy railway station trains leave from platform $5$ every $6 \ \text{minutes}$ and from platform $8$ every 
$10 \ \text{minutes}$. Trains leave from both platforms at $15{:}57$. When do trains next leave both platforms at the same time?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time trains leave both platforms simultaneously }     &= 15{:}57 + 30 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                                            &= 16{:}27 
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$ 16{:}27 $

</div>
</div>

<div class='workingcodeblock'>

$
</code></pre>

<div class='question question'>

How many minutes are there from $11{:}11$ until $23{:}23$ on the same day?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Difference}     &= 23{:}23 - 11{:}11 \\\\
                      &= 12 \ \text{hours} \ 12 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                      &= 12 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 12 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                      &= 720 + 12 \\\\
                      &= 732  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$732  \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Difference}     &= 23{:}23 - 11{:}11 \\\\
                      &= 12 \ \text{hours} \ 12 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                      &= 12 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 12 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                      &= 720 + 12 \\\\
                      &= 732  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Dita watches the television for $2 \text{ hours } 45 \text{ minutes}$ each weekend. How much time does she spend watching television over three weekends?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total time} &= 3 \times 2 \ \text{hours} \ 45 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                  &= 3 \times 2 \ \text{hours} + 3 \times 45 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                  &= 6 \ \text{hours} + 135 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                  &= 6 \ \text{hours} + 2 \ \text{hours} + 15 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                  &= 8 \ \text{hours} \ 15 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$8 \ \text{hours} \ 15 \ \text{minutes}$

                  &= 6 \ \text{hours} + 2 \ \text{hours} + 15 \ \text{minutes} \\\\

\end{aligned}
$

<!-- - if !options[:questions_only] -->
<!-- = render :reviewsubtag, question: question -->
<div class='question question'>

The second hand of a clock points to the number $2$. After one minute and twenty seconds what number will it be pointing to?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{After one minute and twenty seconds} &= 2 + 1 \ \text{minute} \ 20 \ \text{seconds} \\\\
                                           &= 2 + 20 \ \text{seconds} \\\\
                                           &= 2 + \dfrac {20} {5} \\\\
                                           &= 2 + \dfrac {5 \times 4} {5} \\\\
                                           &= 2 + \dfrac {\cancel{5} \times 4} {\cancel{5}} \\\\
                                           &= 2 + 4 \\\\
                                           &= 6
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$6$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{After one minute and twenty seconds} &= 2 + 1 \ \text{minute} \ 20 \ \text{seconds} \\\\
                                           &= 2 + 20 \ \text{seconds} \\\\
                                           &= 2 + \dfrac {20} {5} \\\\
                                           &= 2 + \dfrac {5 \times 4} {5} \\\\
                                           &= 2 + \dfrac {\cancel{5} \times 4} {\cancel{5}} \\\\
                                           &= 2 + 4 \\\\
                                           &= 6
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>

The time in the USA is $5 \ \text{hours}$ behind UK time.

What time is it in Australia when it is $4 \ \text{am}$ in the USA?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {time in UK}         &= 4 \ \text{am} + 5 \ \text{hours} \\\\
                           &= 9 \ \text{am} \\\\
\text {time in Australia}  &= 9 \ \text{am} + 9 \ \text{hours} \\\\
                           &= 6 \ \text{pm}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$6 \ \text{pm}$

</div>
</div>

\text {time in Australia}  &= 9 \ \text{am} + 9 \ \text{hours} \\\\
                           &= 6 \ \text{pm}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

What is the time when the glow from the clock is greatest?

</div>

Greatest glow $= 08{:}08$

<!-- - if !options[:questions_only] -->

<div class='question question'>


The time in Athens is two hours ahead of the time in London.
For example when it is $15{:}00$ in London it is $17{:}00$ in Athens.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working placeholder'>

TODOWORKING

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer placeholder'>

TODOANSWER

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>TODOWORKING
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>TODOANSWER
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Nani flies directly to Athens from London, departing at $08{:}20 \ \text{hours}$. 
If the journey from London takes $3 \ \text{hours } 45 \ \text{minutes}$, at what time, local to Athens, will Nani arrive in Athens?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time to Athens}               &= 08{:}20 + 3 \ \text{hours } 45 \ \text{minutes} + 2 \ \text{hours } \\\\
                                    &= 08{:}20 + 5 \ \text{hours } 45 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                    &= 14{:}05
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$14{:}05$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time to Athens}               &= 08{:}20 + 3 \ \text{hours } 45 \ \text{minutes} + 2 \ \text{hours } \\\\
                                    &= 08{:}20 + 5 \ \text{hours } 45 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                    &= 14{:}05
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$14{:}05$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

It takes the same time to fly from Athens to London as it takes from London to Athens.

Iani leaves Athens airport at $19{:}15 \ \text{hours}$.

At what time will she reach London (local time)?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time Nani arrives in London}     &= 19{:}15 + 3 \ \text{hours } 45 \ \text{minutes} - 2 \ \text{hours } \\\\
                                    &= 19{:}15 + 1 \ \text{hour } 45 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                    &= 21{:}00
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$21{:}00$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

                                    &= 21{:}00
\end{aligned}
$

<!-- = render :reviewsubtag, question: question -->
<div class='question question'>

I arrived at the station at $7.47 \ \text{a.m}$. My train was due at five past eight but was $13 \ \text{minutes}$ late.

How long did I have to wait for my train?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Waiting time} &= 8{:}05 \ \text{am} + 13 \ \text{minutes} - 7{:}47 \ \text{am} \\\\
                    &= 18 \ \text{minutes} + 13 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                    &= 31 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$31 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Waiting time} &= 8{:}05 \ \text{am} + 13 \ \text{minutes} - 7{:}47 \ \text{am} \\\\
                    &= 18 \ \text{minutes} + 13 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                    &= 31 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$31 \ \text{minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
